The yeast <i>Saccharomyces cerevisiae</i> is used in this study. A budding yeast that is homozygous recessive for <span style="color: #57651a;">Gene M</span> colonies are small, dense, and secrete compounds that inhibit the growth of nearby colonies.</li> <li><strong><span style="color: #962f77;">Gene Y</span></strong> is associated with the '<span style="color: #962f77;"><i>yolk</i></span>' phenotype. The yeast <i>Saccharomyces cerevisiae</i> is used in this study. The yeast <i>Saccharomyces cerevisiae</i> is used in this study. A budding yeast that is homozygous recessive for <span style="color: #6d5d00;">Gene E</span> cells absorb excessive amounts of liquid, resulting in giant, swollen cells.</li> <li><strong><span style="color: #7d4190;">Gene F</span></strong> is affiliated with the '<span style="color: #7d4190;"><i>fuzzy</i></span>' phenotype. MC	<p>bcd3_2944</p> <h5>Unordered Tetrad Two Gene Mapping</h5><p>In this problem, you will use unordered tetrads to determine the between a single pair of genes and calculate the distances between them. The yeast <i>Saccharomyces cerevisiae</i> is used in this study. A cross has been performed to study the linkage relationships among two genes, and the resulting genotypes are summarized in the table below.</p><h6>Characteristics of Recessive Phenotypes</h6><p><ul><li><strong><span style="color: #8a4c19;">Gene D</span></strong> is related to the '<span style="color: #8a4c19;"><i>doubled</i></span>' phenotype. A budding yeast that is homozygous recessive for <span style="color: #8a4c19;">Gene D</span> cells display double or multiple budding, with several buds emerging simultaneously.</li> <li><strong><span style="color: #4154b4;">Gene K</span></strong> is correlated with the '<span style="color: #4154b4;"><i>knotted</i></span>' phenotype. A budding yeast that is homozygous recessive for <span style="color: #4154b4;">Gene K</span> cells grow in twisted, coiled shapes, resulting in a knotted or gnarled appearance.</li> </ul></p><table style="border-collapse: collapse;
